## Owner-authorized provider-independent review — 2026-09-18

The owner's later explicit instruction removes the Codex blocking dependency and authorizes a durable provider-independent review policy. This supersedes the earlier same-day plan that required #5422 followed by a four-path `fix/provider-neutral-review-admission-20260918` PR while retaining native Codex/Copilot authority. That earlier plan and its observed provider failures are historical; they are not prerequisites for this owner-authorized migration. MASTER v2.1 R0.2 provider selection is superseded only for development review. Independent review, engineering quality, exact-SHA evidence and production acceptance remain required. Qwen remains confined to Gekta; Gekta inference, models, evaluation and production runtime are unchanged.

The newly authorized migration is one narrow PR on `fix/provider-independent-review-20260918`, limited to its exact scope manifest: review policy and prompts, the deterministic readiness verifier and its regressions, removal of mandatory PR-provider workflow entry points, retirement of automated merge behavior, and the scope enforcement needed for these exact paths. This grants no additional product, database, dependency, deployment or secret-access scope. The migration itself requires actual independent review of its current diff, fresh applicable CI/security and a manual SHA-bound merge under existing GitHub protections. It does not depend on a native AI provider producing a review event.

Current review contract:

- No named AI provider, quota, account plan, model endpoint or hosted review service is a mandatory dependency. Optional AI review findings remain review findings and must be addressed when applicable.
- An independent human or a separate session review agent must inspect the actual exact-head diff. The implementation author cannot supply their own independent review. Record the full head SHA, reviewer identity and independence, reviewed scope, checks, limitations and findings. Session review is recorded as session review, never forged as native GitHub bot or human approval.
- The deterministic engineering-readiness check retains exact-head identity, complete applicable substantive CI/security checks, implementation-owner exact-head audit, active latest `CHANGES_REQUESTED` and unresolved current review-thread blocking. No provider failure can conceal a substantive failure or resolve a finding.
- `--manual-readiness` can return `READY_FOR_MANUAL_REVIEW`; this is a readiness result, not independent-review PASS or merge permission. The default CLI returns `AUTOMATIC_MERGE_DISABLED`. Missing or stale independent review must be resolved through a real reviewer before a manual merge.
- Automated merging and label-based merge authority are disabled. Immediately before a manual merge, the authorized operator verifies the independent review, reloads current head and readiness, and supplies the full expected head SHA. Existing GitHub branch protections apply; no force merge, fabricated checks or approval impersonation is permitted.
- A head change invalidates previous review, owner audit and CI evidence. Provider retirement does not transfer historical PASS, dismiss findings or claim production acceptance.

Required regressions: absent, rate-limited and retired AI providers cannot block otherwise valid manual readiness; readiness never enables automatic merge; missing or stale owner audit, malformed or incomplete check metadata, red/pending substantive CI, active changes-requested and unresolved review threads still block. Preserve meaningful exact-head and workflow-run authority coverage, including the distinction between transport failure and malformed metadata. Negative evidence must not become PASS because a provider is retired.

Execution sequence: independently review and manually merge this owner-authorized migration after fresh applicable CI; verify live main; reassess #5422 and #5406 against that main and preserve their still-needed scope-enforcement and check-run-authority fixes without reintroducing provider binding; then forward-sync #5347, reuse the saved app_outbox transaction-local claim-protocol fixture, and obtain full CI plus real Production-like Kubernetes Acceptance PASS before its independent review and manual SHA-bound merge. Every remaining change stays in a separately approved narrow scope.

IR-20 remains active. Its final closure requires the exact-current-main REG.RU release, verified immutable running images and canonical production Compose topology, functional live acceptance for the touched outbox flow, and at least 30 minutes of observation required by MASTER. Worker publication, protected release and rollback work require their own reviewed scopes and operational evidence. Local patches, independent review, green CI, a Kubernetes PASS, image publication and a merge do not by themselves constitute `PRODUCTION_PASS`. No IR-21 or other product delivery slice opens before the required IR-20 acceptance is complete.

canonical rollback images, and forbids broad prune/delete capabilities.')\n           PY\n \n+      - name: Prove actual bounded reclaim positive and negative scenarios\n+        shell: bash\n+        run: |\n+          python3 - <<'PY_RECLAIM_TEST'\n+          import ast\n+          import copy\n+          import hashlib\n+          import json\n+          import pathlib\n+          import re\n+          import subprocess\n+          import sys\n+          import tempfile\n+          import unittest\n+          from unittest.mock import patch\n+          \n+          SOURCE = 40\n+          GIB_KB = 1024 * 1024\n+          \n+          \n+          def image(component, generation, namespace='transfer', revision=None):\n+              revision = revision or hashlib.sha1(f'{component}-{namespace}-{generation}'.encode()).hexdigest()\n+              image_id = 'sha256:' + hashlib.sha256(f'{component}-{namespace}-{generation}'.encode()).hexdigest()\n+              prefix = f'pc-crop-transfer/{component}' if namespace == 'transfer' else f'ghcr.io/pachaninm-lab/grainflow-{component}'\n+              return {\n+                  'Id': image_id, 'Size': 1024 * 1024,\n+                  'Created': f'2026-09-{generation:02d}T00:00:00.000000000Z',\n+                  'RepoTags': [f'{prefix}:{revision}'],\n+                  'RepoDigests': [f'{prefix}@{image_id}'],\n+                  'Config': {'Labels': {'org.opencontainers.image.revision': revision}},\n+              }\n+          \n+          \n+          class DockerFixture:\n+              def __init__(self, items=None, free=0, gain=6 * GIB_KB, all_become_used=False, changed_refs=False, changed_ref_target=False, removal_error=False, retain_unreferenced=False, parent_chain=False):\n+                  items = items or [image(c, g, n) for c in ('api', 'web', 'migration') for n in ('transfer', 'canonical') for g in (1, 2, 3)]\n+                  self.images = {i['Id']: copy.deepcopy(i) for i in items}\n+                  self.parent_links = {}\n+                  self.unattested_parents = ()\n+                  if parent_chain:\n+                      child = image('web', 1)['Id']\n+                      parent_ids = tuple('sha256:' + hashlib.sha256(f'unproven-parent-{n}'.encode()).hexdigest() for n in (1, 2))\n+                      for parent_id in parent_ids:\n+                          self.images[parent_id] = {'Id': parent_id, 'Size': 0, 'Created': '2026-08-01T00:00:00Z',\n+                                                    'RepoTags': [], 'RepoDigests': [], 'Config': {'Labels': None}}\n+                      self.parent_links = {child: parent_ids[0], parent_ids[0]: parent_ids[1]}\n+                      self.unattested_parents = parent_ids\n+                  self.before = copy.deepcopy(self.images)\n+                  self.containers = {}\n+                  self.free = free\n+                  self.gain = gain\n+                  self.all_become_used = all_become_used\n+                  self.changed_refs = changed_refs\n+                  self.changed_ref_target = changed_ref_target\n+                  self.removal_error = removal_error\n+                  self.retain_unreferenced = retain_unreferenced\n+                  self.ps_calls = 0\n+                  self.ls_done = False\n+                  self.removed_refs = []\n+                  self.deleted = []\n+                  self.all_commands = []\n+          \n+              def resolve(self, ref):\n+                  if ref in self.images:\n+                      return self.images[ref]\n+                  return next((x for x in self.images.values() if ref in (x.get('RepoTags') or []) + (x.get('RepoDigests') or [])), None)\n+          \n+              def run(self, argv, **kwargs):\n+                  self.all_commands.append(argv)\n+                  assert kwargs['stdout'] == subprocess.PIPE and kwargs['stderr'] == subprocess.PIPE\n+                  stdout, stderr, rc = '', '', 0\n+                  if argv[:2] == ['df', '-Pk']:\n+                      stdout = f'Filesystem 1024-blocks Used Available Capacity Mounted on\\n/dev/test 99999999 100 {self.free} 100% /test\\n'\n+                  elif argv == ['docker', 'ps', '-aq', '--no-trunc']:\n+                      self.ps_calls += 1\n+                      if self.all_become_used and self.ps_calls > 1:\n+                          self.containers = {f'container-{i}': k for i, k in enumerate(self.images)}\n+                      stdout = '\\n'.join(self.containers)\n+                  elif argv[:2] == ['docker', 'inspect']:\n+                      stdout = json.dumps([{'Image': self.containers[argv[2]]}])\n+                  elif argv == ['docker', 'image', 'ls', '-q', '--no-trunc']:\n+                      self.ls_done = True\n+                      stdout = '\\n'.join(self.images)\n+                  elif argv[:3] == ['docker', 'image', 'inspect']:\n+                      ref = argv[3]\n+                      row = self.resolve(ref)\n+                      if self.changed_refs and self.ps_calls > 1 and row:\n+                          row['RepoTags'] = ['third-party/unapproved:latest']\n+                      if self.changed_ref_target and not ref.startswith('sha256:') and self.ps_calls > 1 and row:\n+                          row = {**row, 'Id': 'sha256:' + 'e' * 64}\n+                      if row:\n+                          stdout = json.dumps([row])\n+                      else:\n+                          rc, stderr = 1, 'No such image'\n+                  elif argv[:3] == ['docker', 'image', 'rm']:\n+                      arguments = argv[3:]\n+                      no_prune = arguments[:1] == ['--no-prune']\n+                      ref = arguments[-1]\n+                      assert arguments == (['--no-prune', ref] if no_prune else [ref]) and not ref.startswith('-'), argv\n+                      row = self.resolve(ref)\n+                      if self.removal_error:\n+                          rc, stderr = 1, 'no space left on device'\n+                      elif row is None:\n+ ref]\n+                          if ref == row['Id'] or not self.retain_unreferenced and not row['RepoTags'] + row['RepoDigests']:\n+                              self.deleted.append(row['Id'])\n+                              del self.images[row['Id']]\n+                              self.free += self.gain\n+                              # Model Docker's documented automatic deletion of untagged parents.\n+                              parent_id = self.parent_links.get(row['Id'])\n+                              while not no_prune and parent_id in self.images:\n+                                  parent = self.images[parent_id]\n+                                  if parent_id in self.containers.values() or parent['RepoTags'] or parent['RepoDigests']:\n+                                      break\n+                                  self.deleted.append(parent_id)\n+                                  del self.images[parent_id]\n+                                  parent_id = self.parent_links.get(parent_id)\n+                  else:\n+                      raise AssertionError(f'unexpected capability: {argv}')\n+                  return subprocess.CompletedProcess(argv, rc, stdout, stderr)\n+          \n+          \n+          def execute(fixture):\n+              with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n+                  report = pathlib.Path(tmp) / 'report.json'\n+                  argv = ['reclaim', TARGET, '123', str(report), str(5 * GIB_KB), str(6 * GIB_KB), '/test']\n+                  code = 0\n+                  with patch.object(sys, 'argv', argv), patch.object(subprocess, 'run', fixture.run):\n+                      try:\n+                          exec(compile(PAYLOAD, '<actual recovery payload>', 'exec'), {})\n+                      except SystemExit as e:\n+                          code = e.code\n+                  return code, json.loads(report.read_text())\n+          \n+          \n+          class RecoveryTests(unittest.TestCase):\n+              def test_proven_transfer_tag_then_digest_removes_object(self):\n+                  f = DockerFixture([image('web', g) for g in (1, 2, 3)])\n+ self.assertEqual(report['deletedImageCount'], 1)\n+                  self.assertNotIn(image('web', 1)['Id'], f.images)\n+                  self.assertIn(image('web', 2)['Id'], f.images)\n+ in (1, 2, 3)], retain_unreferenced=True)\n+                  code, report = execute(f)\n+                  self.assertEqual(code, 0)\n+                  self.assertEqual(report['removedReferenceCount'], 2)\n+                  self.assertEqual(report['deletedImageCount'], 1)\n+                  self.assertEqual(f.removed_refs[-1], image('web', 1)['Id'])\n+          \n+              def assert_unattested_parent_chain_survives(self, retain_unreferenced):\n+                  f = DockerFixture([image('web', g) for g in (1, 2, 3)],\n+ self.assertEqual(f.deleted, [image('web', 1)['Id']])\n+                  for parent_id in f.unattested_parents:\n+                      self.assertEqual(f.images.get(parent_id), f.before[parent_id])\n+                  self.assertIn(image('web', 2)['Id'], f.images)\n+                  self.assertIn(image('web', 3)['Id'], f.images)\n+                  if retain_unreferenced:\n+                      self.assertEqual(f.removed_refs[-1], image('web', 1)['Id'])\n+                  else:\n+                      self.assertFalse(f.removed_refs[-1].startswith('sha256:'))\n+          \n+              def test_reference_removal_preserves_unattested_parent_chain(self):\n+                  self.assert_unattested_parent_chain_survives(retain_unreferenced=False)\n+          \n+              def test_immutable_id_removal_preserves_unattested_parent_chain(self):\n+                  self.assert_unattested_parent_chain_survives(retain_unreferenced=True)\n+          \n+              def test_actual_payload_stops_at_target_and_preserves_both_reserves(self):\n+                  f = DockerFixture()\n+                  code, report = execute(f)\n+                  self.assertEqual(code, 0)\n+                  self.assertEqual(report['deletedImageCount'], 1)\n+                  self.assertEqual(report['afterAvailableKb'], 6 * GIB_KB)\n+                  self.assertTrue(report['passed'])\n+                  self.assertEqual(report['eligibleImageCount'], 6)\n+                  for c in ('api', 'web', 'migration'):\n+                      for n in ('canonical', 'transfer'):\n+                          for g in (2, 3):\n+                              self.assertIn(image(c, g, n)['Id'], f.images)\n+          \n+              def test_no_removal_when_capacity_already_sufficient(self):\n+                  f = DockerFixture(free=6 * GIB_KB)\n+                  _, report = execute(f)\n+                  self.assertEqual(f.removed_refs, [])\n+                  self.assertEqual(report['attemptedImageCount'], 0)\n+          \n+              def self.assertEqual(code, 91)\n+                  self.assertEqual(f.removed_refs, [])\n+                  self.assertEqual(report['removeFailureCounts']['CONTAINER_REFERENCE'], 6)\n+          \n+ self.assertEqual(code, 91)\n+                  self.assertEqual(f.removed_refs, [])\n+                  self.assertEqual(report['removeFailureCounts']['UNEXPECTED_REFERENCE_STATE'], 6)\n+          \n+              def test_reference_now_resolves_to_different_image_fails_closed(self):\n+                  f = DockerFixture(changed_ref_target=True)\n+                  code, report = execute(f)\n+ +  React.useEffect(() => {
```diff
+    // Next client navigation retains the root layout. Only a matching standalone
+    // route may update document language; embedded chat keeps its host's locale.
+    if (window.location.pathname !== GEKTA_PATHS[locale]) return;
+    const root = document.documentElement;
+    const language = locale === 'zh' ? 'zh-CN' : locale;
+    const previousLanguage = root.getAttribute('lang');
+    const contentLanguage = document.querySelector<HTMLMetaElement>('meta[http-equiv="Content-Language" i]');
+    const previousContentLanguage = contentLanguage?.getAttribute('content') ?? null;
+    root.setAttribute('lang', language);
+    contentLanguage?.setAttribute('content', language);
+    return () => {
+      // A newly mounted page may already own these attributes. Never overwrite it.
+      if (root.getAttribute('lang') === language) {
+        if (previousLanguage === null) root.removeAttribute('lang');
+        else root.setAttribute('lang', previousLanguage);
+      }
+      if (contentLanguage?.getAttribute('content') === language) {
+        if (previousContentLanguage === null) contentLanguage.removeAttribute('content');
+        else contentLanguage.setAttribute('content', previousContentLanguage);
+      }
+    };
+  }, [locale]);
diff --git a/apps/web/tests/unit/gektaVoiceAndLegal.test.ts b/apps/web/tests/unit/gektaVoiceAndLegal.test.ts
index 7c1986cb5..44a791388 100644
--- a/apps/web/tests/unit/gektaVoiceAndLegal.test.ts
+++ b/apps/web/tests/unit/gektaVoiceAndLegal.test.ts
@@ -3 +3,2 @@ import { resolve } from 'node:path';
-import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+import { cleanup, render, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
@@ -164,0 +166,74 @@ describe('Gekta interface language inheritance', () => {
+
+
+describe('Gekta standalone document language lifecycle', () => {
+  let meta: HTMLMetaElement;
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    window.localStorage.clear();
+    document.documentElement.lang = 'ru';
+    meta = document.createElement('meta');
+    meta.httpEquiv = 'Content-Language';
+    meta.content = 'ru';
+    document.head.appendChild(meta);
+    vi.spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch').mockImplementation(async () => new Response('{}', { status: 401 }));
+  });
+  afterEach(() => {
+    cleanup();
+    meta.remove();
+    window.history.replaceState(null, '', '/');
+    document.documentElement.lang = 'ru';
+    vi.restoreAllMocks();
+  });
+
+  it('updates the actual mounted workspace and root on RU -> EN -> ZH -> RU client navigation', async () => {
+    window.history.replaceState(null, '', '/gekta');
+    const view = render(createElement(GektaChatWorkspace, { locale: 'ru' }));
+    for (const [locale, pathname, language] of [
+      ['en', '/gekta/en', 'en'], ['zh', '/gekta/zh', 'zh-CN'], ['ru', '/gekta', 'ru'],
+    ] as const) {
+      window.history.replaceState(null, '', pathname);
+      view.rerender(createElement(GektaChatWorkspace, { locale }));
+      await waitFor(() => expect(document.documentElement.lang).toBe(language));
+      expect(meta.content).toBe(language);
+      expect(view.container.querySelector('textarea')?.closest('[lang]')?.getAttribute('lang')).toBe(language);
+    }
+  });
+
+  it.each(['/platform-v7', '/gekta/console', '/gekta/register', '/gekta/en/price', '/gekta/en-extra', '/gekta'])
+  ('never changes platform or mismatched document language at %s', (pathname) => {
+    window.history.replaceState(null, '', pathname);
+    render(createElement(GektaChatWorkspace, { locale: 'en' }));
+    expect(document.documentElement.lang).toBe('ru');
+    expect(meta.content).toBe('ru');
+  });
+
+  it('restores pre-existing document metadata when the standalone workspace unmounts', async () => {
+    window.history.replaceState(null, '', '/gekta/en');
+    const view = render(createElement(GektaChatWorkspace, { locale: 'en' }));
+    await waitFor(() => expect(document.documentElement.lang).toBe('en'));
+    view.unmount();
+    expect(document.documentElement.lang).toBe('ru');
+    expect(meta.content).toBe('ru');
+  });
+
+  it('does not overwrite metadata acquired by a newer page owner', async () => {
+    window.history.replaceState(null, '', '/gekta/zh');
+    const view = render(createElement(GektaChatWorkspace, { locale: 'zh' }));
+    await waitFor(() => expect(document.documentElement.lang).toBe('zh-CN'));
+    document.documentElement.lang = 'fr';
+    meta.content = 'fr';
+    view.unmount();
+    expect(document.documentElement.lang).toBe('fr');
+    expect(meta.content).toBe('fr');
+  });
+
+  it('preserves absent metadata instead of inventing a head element or an old lang', async () => {
+    window.history.replaceState(null, '', '/gekta/en');
+    meta.remove();
+    document.documentElement.removeAttribute('lang');
+    const view = render(createElement(GektaChatWorkspace, { locale: 'en' }));
+    await waitFor(() => expect(document.documentElement.lang).toBe('en'));
+    expect(document.head.querySelector('meta[http-equiv="Content-Language"]')).toBeNull();
+    view.unmount();
+    expect(document.documentElement.hasAttribute('lang')).toBe(false);
+  });
+});
```
